Per-button specification — all limit orders — per-button independent ▲▼
Row Button Label template Default buffer Range Execution semantics Own field
Row 1
Exits		 Trim ▲▼ (left) "Trim +{N}" / "Trim" / "Trim -{N}" +1 −5 to +5 ▲ market → limit. Sells ceil(pos/2) at bid + _trimBuffer × tick (long) or ask − _trimBuffer × tick (short). Auto-detects direction from live acc.Positions[instr].MarketPosition. Own ▲▼ right side. volatile int _trimBuffer = 1
Row 1
Exits		 Flatten ▲▼ (right) "Flatten +{N}" / "Flatten" / "Flatten -{N}" +1 −5 to +5 ▲ market → limit. Sells full position qty at bid + _flattenBuffer × tick (long) or buys at ask − _flattenBuffer × tick (short). Auto-detects direction. Own ▲▼ right side, independent of Trim. volatile int _flattenBuffer = 1
Row 2
Mgmt		 BE ▲▼ (left) "BE +{N}" / "BE" / "BE -{N}" +1 −10 to +10 Move all working stop orders to avgEntry + _beBuffer × tickSize. Own ▲▼ right side.
Armed state (ArmPendingBe active): amber background — "waiting for trigger price."
Fired state: green flash 500ms, then restores NTButtonStyle.
CONNECTED: blue border — ▲▼ reprices live stop immediately via MoveStopToBreakEven(rule, newBuffer). Exits when position closes. 		volatile int _beBuffer = 1
Row 2
Mgmt		 Cancel (right) "Cancel" — — Cancel all working entry orders on all accounts. No buffer, no ▲▼. ATM bracket legs always protected (Layer 1 guard). Enabled only when working entry exists on any account in rule. No field — plain button
Row 3
Toggle		 ● COPY ON/OFF (full width) "● COPY ON" / "◯ COPY OFF" — — Global copy enable/disable. Full-width (spans both columns). Green when ON, dim when OFF. No buffer, no ▲▼. bool in CopyEngine
Sign convention — buffer always means "better than a market order":
Exit (Trim / Flatten) — positive buffer = MORE ticks in your favour vs hitting market:
  Long exit: Sell Limit @ bid + buffer×tick → at buffer=1, you sell at the ask instead of the bid. You gain the spread.
  Short exit: Buy Limit @ ask − buffer×tick → at buffer=1, you buy at the bid instead of the ask. You gain the spread.
  Example: Long MES at 5002, price at 5003 (bid=5002.75, ask=5003.00), Trim buffer=+1 → sell limit at 5003.00. Market sell would give 5002.75. You get 1 extra tick = $1.25.

Scotty's "Bid+1" / "Ask−1" buttons are ENTRY buttons (different action, same formula):
  Long entry Bid+1: Buy Limit @ bid + 1 tick = ask price = aggressive buy, fills immediately at inside spread.
  Short entry Ask−1: Sell Limit @ ask − 1 tick = bid price = aggressive sell, fills immediately at inside spread.
  Same math, opposite action (Buy vs Sell). Exit buffer improves your fill. Entry buffer makes it more aggressive.

Flatten / Trim direction auto-detection: isLong = pos.MarketPosition == MarketPosition.Long → Long: Sell Limit @ bid + buffer. Short: Buy Limit @ ask − buffer. Flat: skip silently. Buffer=0 → limit at the raw bid/ask (same result as aggressive market order). Buffer=1 → 1 tick improvement. Buffer=2 → 2 ticks. Trim uses identical direction logic. 
// ── TradeCopierPanel.cs — Per-button independent ▲▼ (B12 FINAL) ──────────
// 3 independent buffer fields. Each button owns its own ▲▼. NT theme via NTButtonStyle.
// volatile int — UI thread only, no cross-thread mutation. No lock() required.
// Toggle (Row 3) has no buffer. Cancel (Row 2 right) has no buffer.
private volatile int _trimBuffer     = 1;  // Row 1 left
private volatile int _flattenBuffer  = 1;  // Row 1 right
private volatile int _beBuffer       = 1;  // Row 2 left (amber armed / green fired / blue connected)

// Label helper — CYC=1 (pure format, shared by all 3 buffered buttons)
private static string FormatBuffer(string name, int ticks)
    => ticks == 0 ? name : $"{name} {(ticks > 0 ? "+" : "")}{ticks}";
// FormatBuffer("Trim", 1)    → "Trim +1"
// FormatBuffer("BE", 0)      → "BE"   (blank-when-0 rule: 0 is floor, very common)
// FormatBuffer("Flatten", 0) → "Flatten"  (buffers 0..∞ only — never negative)

// ── ROW 1 LEFT — Trim — own ▲▼ ──────────────────────────────────────────
private void OnTrimUp(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { _trimBuffer++; _trimLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("Trim", _trimBuffer); }
private void OnTrimDown(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { if (_trimBuffer > 0) _trimBuffer--; _trimLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("Trim", _trimBuffer); }
private void OnTrimClicked(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    => CopyEngine.Instance?.Trim(_rule, _trimBuffer);

// ── ROW 1 RIGHT — Flatten — own ▲▼ ──────────────────────────────────────
private void OnFlattenUp(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { _flattenBuffer++; _flattenLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("Flatten", _flattenBuffer); }
private void OnFlattenDown(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { if (_flattenBuffer > 0) _flattenBuffer--; _flattenLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("Flatten", _flattenBuffer); }
private void OnFlattenClicked(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    => CopyEngine.Instance?.Flatten(_rule, _flattenBuffer);

// ── ROW 2 LEFT — BE — own ▲▼ + 3-state machine: IDLE→ARMED→CONNECTED ──────
// _beArmed:     price hasn't crossed entry+buffer yet. Amber glow. Auto-fires when it does.
// _beConnected: stop moved successfully. Blue border. ▲▼ reprices live stop immediately.
// These two booleans are MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE — never both true simultaneously.
private volatile bool _beArmed     = false;
private volatile bool _beConnected = false;

// ▲ uncapped — buffer goes 0..∞. No ceiling. RepeatButton Delay=400 Interval=100.
// While CONNECTED, ▲ = stop moves to avgEntry + new_buffer × tick immediately.
private void OnBeUp(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    _beBuffer++;
    _beLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("BE", _beBuffer);
    if (_beConnected) CopyEngine.Instance?.BreakEven(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer);  // live reprice
}
// ▼ floor=0 — never negative. ▼ while ARMED changes pending buffer only (no re-arm).
private void OnBeDown(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    if (_beBuffer > 0) _beBuffer--;
    _beLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("BE", _beBuffer);
    if (_beConnected) CopyEngine.Instance?.BreakEven(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer);  // live reprice
}

// BE click — 3 paths based on current state (CYC=4)
// CONNECTED means: BE already fired once, stop is live at entry+N. Blue border.
// While CONNECTED, every click = BreakEven(instrument, ++_beBuffer) — stop climbs by 1 tick each time.
// Long 5002, BE+2 → stop 5002.50. Click again → BE+3 → stop 5002.75. Click again → BE+4 → stop 5003.00.
// ▲ does the same thing. ▲▲▲ = 3 more clicks. Click = identical to one ▲ press while CONNECTED.
private void OnBeClicked(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    if (!CopyEngine.Instance?.HasOpenPosition(_rule.Instrument) ?? true) return;  // no pos: no-op
    if (_beArmed)
    {
        // Second click while armed = cancel arm (escape hatch) → IDLE
        SetBeArmed(false);
        CopyEngine.Instance?.CancelBeArm(_rule.Instrument);
        return;
    }
    if (_beConnected)
    {
        // CONNECTED: click = same as ▲ — increment buffer, move stop to entry + new buffer
        // stop = avgEntry + (++_beBuffer) × tick. No bid/ask needed — pure entry-price math.
        _beBuffer++;
        _beLabel.Text = FormatBuffer("BE", _beBuffer);
        CopyEngine.Instance?.BreakEven(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer);
        return;
    }
    // IDLE: arm-or-fire based on whether price has already crossed entry+buffer
    // If already past entry+buffer → fire immediately → CONNECTED
    // If not yet there → ARM (amber) → engine price-watch → auto-fires on cross
    bool canFireNow = CopyEngine.Instance?.IsPriceAtBe(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer) ?? false;
    if (canFireNow)
    {
        CopyEngine.Instance?.BreakEven(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer);
        SetBeConnected(true);
    }
    else
    {
        CopyEngine.Instance?.ArmBeWatch(_rule.Instrument, _beBuffer);  // engine starts price watch
        SetBeArmed(true);
    }
}
// SetBeArmed(bool): amber background when armed; restores NTButtonStyle when idle
// SetBeConnected(bool): blue border when connected; also clears _beArmed
// PositionStateChanged(hasPos=false): calls SetBeConnected(false) + SetBeArmed(false)

// BE visual state — called from CopyEngine event (Dispatcher.InvokeAsync)
internal void SetBeArmed(bool armed)
{
    _beArmed = armed;
    _beButton.Background = armed
        ? new SolidColorBrush(NTBrushes.Amber)   // armed: amber — waiting for price
        : (Brush)DependencyProperty.UnsetValue; // off: restore NTButtonStyle
}
// BE fired → green flash 500ms then restore NTButtonStyle
internal async void FlashBeFired()  // async void OK: UI event handler, non-hot-path
{
    _beButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(NTBrushes.Green);
    await Task.Delay(500);
    _beButton.Background = (Brush)DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
}
// ── ROW 2 RIGHT — Cancel — no buffer ────────────────────────────────────────
// Scope: Working + Accepted ENTRY orders ONLY (OrderState.Working / .Accepted).
// NEVER touches StopMarket or LimitIfTouched bracket legs — those are ATM-owned.
private void OnCancelClicked(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
    => CopyEngine.Instance?.CancelPendingEntries(_rule.Instrument);

// All handlers: CYC ≤ 4. No lock(). async void only in FlashBeFired. JS-compliant.

        
        
// ── CopyEngine.cs — B12 method signatures ────────────────────────────────
// Trim/Flatten: market → limit@bid/ask. Buffer=+1 default (1 tick better than bid/ask).
// PTT-prefix guard: orders named "PTT-*" skip OnOrderUpdate copy chain (no double-dispatch).

// Trim — exit ceil(pos/2) at Limit@bid+exitBuffer (long) or ask-exitBuffer (short)
// B12 CHANGE: Market → Limit order with buffer. Existing Trim(Instrument) becomes Trim(Instrument, int).
// Long exit: Sell Limit @ bid+buffer = fills at ask when buffer=1 (1 tick better than market sell).
// Short exit: Buy Limit @ ask-buffer = fills at bid when buffer=1 (1 tick better than market buy).
// CYC=2 outer + TrimOnAccount helper CYC=4
internal void Trim(Instrument instrument, int exitBuffer)
{
    if (instrument == null) return;
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
        TrimOnAccount(acc, instrument, exitBuffer);
}

// Flatten — exit full position at Limit@bid+exitBuffer (long) or ask-exitBuffer (short)
// Same Limit logic as Trim. B12 CHANGE: Market → Limit.
// CYC=2 outer + FlattenOnAccount helper CYC=4
internal void Flatten(Instrument instrument, int exitBuffer)
{
    if (instrument == null) return;
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
        FlattenOnAccount(acc, instrument, exitBuffer);
}

// TrimOnAccount / FlattenOnAccount helper (CYC=4 each) — B12 NEW:
// var pos = FindPosition(acc, instr); if (IsFlat(pos)) return;
// bool isLong = pos.MarketPosition == MarketPosition.Long;
// double tick = instr.MasterInstrument.TickSize;
// double lmt = isLong ? bid + exitBuffer*tick : ask - exitBuffer*tick;
// lmt = Math.Round(lmt / tick) * tick;  // snap to tick grid
// int qty = (Trim only) (int)Math.Ceiling(pos.Quantity / 2.0);
// acc.CreateOrder(instr, isLong ? Sell : BuyToCover, Limit, lmt, qty, "PTT-Trim"/"PTT-Flatten");
// NOTE: bid/ask sourced from _lastBid/_lastAsk volatile fields updated on price events.
// Sim101 gate DW-B12-SIM-01 confirms bid/ask are current at click time.

// MoveStopToBreakEven — gains explicit bufferTicks param (was 0-implied)
// CYC=2 outer + MoveStopToBeOnAccount helper CYC=4
internal void MoveStopToBreakEven(CopyRule rule, int bufferTicks)
{
    if (rule == null) return;
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(rule))
        MoveStopToBeOnAccount(acc, rule.Instrument, bufferTicks);
}

// HasOpenPosition — checks master account position for this instrument (CYC=3)
// Uses existing FindPosition(acc, instr) — already in B9 source. AllAccounts(Instrument).
internal bool HasOpenPosition(Instrument instrument)
{
    if (instrument == null) return false;
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        var pos = FindPosition(acc, instrument);
        if (pos != null && pos.Quantity > 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// IsPriceAtBe — true if price already crossed entry+buffer. Uses OrderUpdate tick data. (CYC=4)
// Long:  avgEntry + bufferTicks×tick ≤ lastPrice  →  stop can fire immediately
// Short: avgEntry - bufferTicks×tick ≥ lastPrice  →  stop can fire immediately
// lastPrice = most recent OnOrderUpdate price (NT8 AddOn has no direct bid/ask API).
// Sim101 gate DW-B12-SIM-BE-ARMED-01 must verify this works before B12 execution.
internal bool IsPriceAtBe(Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks)
{
    if (instrument == null) return false;
    var rule = FindRule(instrument);
    if (rule == null) return false;
    var pos = FindPosition(rule.Value.MasterAccount, instrument);
    if (pos == null || pos.Quantity == 0) return false;
    double tick = instrument.MasterInstrument.TickSize;
    double target = pos.AveragePrice
        + (pos.MarketPosition == MarketPosition.Long ? 1 : -1) * bufferTicks * tick;
    return pos.MarketPosition == MarketPosition.Long
        ? _lastPrice >= target   // long: price must be AT or ABOVE entry+buffer
        : _lastPrice <= target;  // short: price must be AT or BELOW entry-buffer
}
// _lastPrice: volatile double field updated in OnOrderUpdate / price event subscription.
// Sim101 required — verify lastPrice is current enough for ARMED check at click time.

// ArmBeWatch — store armed state for engine's price-cross check (CYC=2)
// New fields: volatile Instrument _armedInstrument; volatile int _armedBuffer;
// Engine calls IsPriceAtBe on next price event. On true: BreakEven(instr,buf) + RaiseBeConnected().
internal void ArmBeWatch(Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks)
{
    if (instrument == null) return;
    _armedInstrument = instrument;
    _armedBuffer = bufferTicks;
}
// CancelBeArm — user escape-hatches (second click while ARMED) → clears watch
internal void CancelBeArm(Instrument instrument)
    { if (_armedInstrument == instrument) _armedInstrument = null; }

// BreakEven already exists in B9 source (line 951). No new outer method needed.
// internal void BreakEven(Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks) — iterates AllAccounts(instrument)
// Math: stop = Math.Round((avgEntry + direction×bufferTicks×tick) / tick) × tick
// Long 5002 BE+2 MES: stop = Math.Round((5002 + 2×0.25)/0.25)×0.25 = 5002.50 ✓

// All B12 BE methods: CYC ≤ 4. No lock(). No return null. JS-compliant.
// New fields needed in CopyEngine: _armedInstrument, _armedBuffer, _lastPrice (volatile).

// ── OnOrderUpdate gate chain — PTT-prefix skip guard (new in B12) ────────
// Gate 0: if order.Name starts with "PTT-", return early — prevents double-dispatch
// of PTT-generated Trim/Flatten/BE orders back through the copy chain.
// All existing gates follow unchanged: enabled? source? submitted? dedup? P&L?
if (order.Name != null && order.Name.StartsWith("PTT-")) return;  // Gate 0 — self-skip



        
        

          ⚠ Cancel button scope (B12 LOCKED):
          
    
            
  • Cancels: OrderState.Working and OrderState.Accepted entry orders only — pending limit or stop-limit entries waiting to fill.
    • 
            
  • NEVER touches: StopMarket or LimitIfTouched bracket legs (stop-loss, profit target). ATM-owned — must not be cancelled here.
    • 
            
  • Rationale: Cancelling a bracket stop by mistake = unprotected open position. ATM owns its orders. We cancel only what we placed as entries.
    • 
          

        

        
        

          NT8 WPF compatibility — RepeatButton + theme inheritance:
          
    
            
  • RepeatButton: System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.RepeatButton — standard WPF .NET 4.8, fully available in NT8 AddOn context. Delay=400ms, Interval=100ms — matches NT8's native qty spinner feel.
    • 
            
  • Arrows: Safe pattern: Content = "\u25B2" / "\u25BC" in C# — never literal ▲▼ (ASCII-only mandate).
    • 
            
  • NT8 theme inheritance (YES — fully supported):
              Set Style="{StaticResource NTButtonStyle}" on all Button and RepeatButton instances. NT8 exposes its own WPF ResourceDictionary (NTButtonStyle, NTBrushes, NTTextBoxStyle) — the same keys used by ChartTrader itself. When the user changes the NT8 theme (dark/light/custom), our buttons inherit the new colors, fonts, borders, and hover states automatically. Zero custom XAML styles required. This is the correct approach per the spec's design pillar: "Zero custom styles. We reference NT's own keys."
    • 
            
  • BE armed/fired state overrides: The armed (amber) and fired (green flash) states use _beButton.Background = new SolidColorBrush(NTBrushes.Amber) set via Dispatcher.InvokeAsync. This overrides the NTButtonStyle background only for the BE button state — all other buttons always follow the NT theme. The flash resets to DependencyProperty.UnsetValue (restores NTButtonStyle) after 500ms.
    • 
          

        


        
        

          
✓ LOCKED — Full panel button map (B12 FINAL — 3 rows)

          
Row 1 (exits):   [Trim +1    [▲][▼]]  [Flatten +1  [▲][▼]]   ← _trimBuffer=1, _flattenBuffer=1
                  both: limit @ bid+N (long) / ask-N (short). auto-detect direction.

Row 2 (mgmt):    [BE +1       [▲][▼]]  [Cancel           ]   ← BE _beBuffer=1, Cancel plain
                  BE: amber=ARMED (waiting for price cross). green flash=FIRED (stop moved). blue border=CONNECTED.
                  Click again while CONNECTED = BUMP STOP to current bid/ask-buffer (manual trail step). B13=auto-trail.

            3 independent ▲▼ pairs. 3 volatile int fields. Toggle at bottom.

Live Hook — Buttons stay connected to live orders/stops (DW-B12-BE-HOOK-01, DW-B12-ENTRY-HOOK-01)


          

            Once a button has acted on a live position or working order, it enters
            CONNECTED state. In CONNECTED state, pressing
            ▲▼ reprices the stop or working order immediately — the button becomes a live dial,
            not a one-shot trigger. Buttons stay connected until the position closes or the order fills/cancels.
          


          
          

            
              
ButtonTrigger → CONNECTED▲ while CONNECTED▼ while CONNECTEDExit CONNECTEDVisual

            
            
              

                BE
                User clicks BE (stop moves successfully) and position is open
                Calls MoveStopToBreakEven(rule, ++_beBuffer) — stop moves UP immediately to entry + new ticks
                Calls MoveStopToBreakEven(rule, --_beBuffer) — stop moves DOWN
                PositionStateChanged(hasPos=false) — position closed
                Blue/accent border when CONNECTED. Amber when ArmPendingBe waiting. Green flash when stop moves.
              

              

                Trim
                No CONNECTED mode — one-shot exit. Always uses latest buffer on click.
                —
              

              

                Flatten
                No CONNECTED mode — one-shot exit.
                —
              

            
          


          
          

            
BE full flow — armed → connected → re-click

            

              Example: Long MES at 5002, BE +2, price was at 4998.

              1. Click BE +2. Price below entry+2 (5002.50) → ARMED (amber). No stop move yet.

              2. Price recovers to 5002.50. Engine detects cross → BreakEven(instr, 2) fires → stop = 5002.50. Green flash. Button → CONNECTED (blue).

              3. Press ▲ → buffer becomes 3 → BreakEven(instr, 3) → stop = 5002.75.

              4. Click BE button again while CONNECTED → same as ▲: buffer becomes 4 → stop = 5003.00.

              5. Each click or ▲ while CONNECTED adds 1 more tick above entry. No bid/ask needed — pure entry-price math.


              BreakEven() already exists in B9 source. Math: stop = avgEntry + bufferTicks × tickSize (rounded to grid).

B12 — Full-Panel Mode (side-by-side NT8 + PTT) — Backlog / Optional
A TradeCopierFullPanel that replaces the ChartTrader content area entirely, replicating NT8's Buy/Sell/Account/ATM controls (from scratch, using Account.CreateOrder()) side-by-side with the PTT controls. The replicated buttons have the exact same market access as NT8's native buttons — same API, same result. ATM gap fix = one line: signalName = cbxStrategySelector.SelectedValue.

Decision: Injected-row approach is primary. Full-panel is B12+ optional scope, only if user needs a cleaner visual layout or the collapsed view is insufficient. Not ticketed. No implementation spec yet.
Backlog — not ticketed ATM gap fix = 1 line (signalName passthrough) Same Account.CreateOrder() API — not mimicry

Sell Bid = go short via Sell Limit at Bid + bufferTicks×tick (buffer 0 = at bid; positive = above bid = passive; negative = below bid = aggressive). Default buffer = 0 for both. ▲▼ spinners, range −10/+10. Intent: better price than a market order. All accounts in rule simultaneously. B12 complete (panel layout locked)

B12+ — Live Risk $ + ATR % Input Row (DW-B12-RISK-ATR-INPUTS-01)
The two core variables of the ATR risk engine — Max Risk per Trade ($) and ATR Fraction (%) — are live-editable from the panel, no settings menu required. A single bottom row with two composite spinners, each matching the buffered-button ▲▼ pattern already in B12. Values apply on focus-lost (Tab, click-away, Enter). No separate Apply button.
Two-ceiling formula (locked):
stopTicks = ceil(ATR × atrFraction) × ticksPerPoint
qty = ceil(maxRisk ÷ (stopTicks × tickDollarValue))
MES: tickDollarValue=$1.25, ticksPerPoint=4. atrFraction default=0.75, maxRisk default=$200.

         └─ RepeatButton "\u25BC" → _maxRiskDollars -= 25

  (identical structure for ATR % — step 0.05)

  Risk $: Math.Clamp(v, 10.0, 1000.0)

// ── TradeCopierPanel.cs — Risk $ + ATR % input spinners ────────────────
// Two volatile double fields. UI-thread only. No lock() required.
private volatile double _maxRiskDollars = 200.0;  // range: 10–1000, step: 25
private volatile double _atrFraction    = 0.75;   // range: 0.25–3.00, step: 0.05

// ── Risk $ spinner ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ▲: increment by $25, clamp to 1000. ▼: decrement by $25, clamp to 10.
// Both ▲▼ update TextBox text immediately (no focus-lost needed for spinners).
private void OnRiskUp(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    _maxRiskDollars = Math.Min(_maxRiskDollars + 25.0, 1000.0);
    _riskTextBox.Text = _maxRiskDollars.ToString("F0");
    NotifyRiskChanged();
}
private void OnRiskDown(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    _maxRiskDollars = Math.Max(_maxRiskDollars - 25.0, 10.0);
    _riskTextBox.Text = _maxRiskDollars.ToString("F0");
    NotifyRiskChanged();
}
// LostFocus: parse + clamp + update engine (CYC=3)
private void OnRiskLostFocus(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    if (double.TryParse(_riskTextBox.Text, out double v))
        _maxRiskDollars = Math.Clamp(v, 10.0, 1000.0);
    _riskTextBox.Text = _maxRiskDollars.ToString("F0");  // restore/clamp display
    NotifyRiskChanged();
}

// ── ATR % spinner ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ▲: +0.05, clamp to 3.00. ▼: -0.05, clamp to 0.25.
private void OnAtrFractionUp(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    _atrFraction = Math.Min(Math.Round(_atrFraction + 0.05, 2), 3.0);
    _atrTextBox.Text = _atrFraction.ToString("F2");
    NotifyAtrFractionChanged();
}
private void OnAtrFractionDown(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    _atrFraction = Math.Max(Math.Round(_atrFraction - 0.05, 2), 0.25);
    _atrTextBox.Text = _atrFraction.ToString("F2");
    NotifyAtrFractionChanged();
}
// LostFocus: parse + clamp + update engine (CYC=3)
private void OnAtrFractionLostFocus(object s, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    if (double.TryParse(_atrTextBox.Text, out double v))
        _atrFraction = Math.Clamp(Math.Round(v, 2), 0.25, 3.00);
    _atrTextBox.Text = _atrFraction.ToString("F2");
    NotifyAtrFractionChanged();
}

// NotifyRiskChanged / NotifyAtrFractionChanged — push new values to AtrEngine
// CopyEngine.Instance?.AtrEngine?.SetRiskParams(_maxRiskDollars, _atrFraction);
// AtrEngine recalculates qty on next Calc() call. No live-trade reprice needed —
// values apply to the NEXT entry order, not an open position. CYC=1 each.
private void NotifyRiskChanged()
    => CopyEngine.Instance?.AtrEngine?.SetMaxRisk(_maxRiskDollars);
private void NotifyAtrFractionChanged()
    => CopyEngine.Instance?.AtrEngine?.SetAtrFraction(_atrFraction);

// All handlers: CYC ≤ 3. No lock(). No return null. No async void.
// Math.Clamp: available in .NET 4.8 via Math.Min/Max chain (NT8 safe).
// NT8 note: Math.Clamp may not be available in .NET 4.8 — use Math.Max(Math.Min(v,max),min).
NT8 / .NET 4.8 compatibility:
  • Math.Clamp: Not available in .NET 4.8. Use Math.Max(Math.Min(v, max), min) instead. Pattern already shown in code above.
  • TextBox style: Style="{StaticResource NTTextBoxStyle}" — NT8 exposes this key in its WPF ResourceDictionary. Inherits NT theme (dark/light) automatically.
  • RepeatButton style: Style="{StaticResource NTButtonStyle}" — same as all other ▲▼ spinners in the panel.
  • ASCII in .cs: All arrows use "\u25B2" / "\u25BC". Label text uses ASCII digits only. No Unicode in string literals.
  • volatile double: BANNED in NT8 (NT8-003). These fields are UI-thread-only (panel is on Dispatcher thread). Use plain double (not volatile). No cross-thread access — panel reads/writes on Dispatcher only, AtrEngine reads the value via the Notify push (also dispatched). Plain double is correct here.

ATR % handlers (Up/Down/LostFocus) TradeCopierPanel.cs ~18 CYC ≤ 3 each Clamp 0.25–3.00. Step 0.05. Math.Round(v, 2) after each step.
AtrEngine.SetMaxRisk(double) AtrRiskEngine.cs ~3 CYC=1 Stores value. Recalculated on next Calc() call. No live-position reprice.
AtrEngine.SetAtrFraction(double) AtrRiskEngine.cs ~3 CYC=1 Stores value. Applies to next two-ceiling calculation.

Sim101 gate (DW-B12-RISK-ATR-SIM-01):
  • DW-B12-RISK-ATR-SIM-01 (low): Change Risk $ to 150 via spinner ▲▼. Verify AtrEngine.Calc() on next entry uses 150, not 200. Change ATR % to 0.50. Verify qty formula uses 0.50 multiplier. Both should apply immediately without panel restart.
  • DW-B12-RISK-ATR-SIM-02 (low): Type invalid input (e.g. "abc") in Risk $ box. Tab away. Confirm field resets to last valid value (no crash, no NaN).
  • DW-B12-RISK-ATR-SIM-03 (low): Type 9999 in Risk $ box. Tab away. Confirm value clamps to 1000. Type 0 in ATR % box. Tab away. Confirm value clamps to 0.25.

Agent Readiness — Infrastructure Track
What this is: The Factory Agent Readiness rubric scores a codebase across 9 categories against 82 criteria. The v12 monorepo scores 100% (Level 6). The PTT copier sub-project lives inside that monorepo but has its own infrastructure ceiling — NT8 is a closed desktop process, not a deployable web service. This section tracks the PTT-specific gap, what is genuinely blocked by NT8's architecture, and the concrete deliverables that close each actionable gap.
Current score — PTT Copier sub-project

NT8 structural ceiling
The 5% ceiling gap is not a code problem — it is NT8's deployment model. NT8 compiles C# files internally via its own Roslyn host inside a closed desktop process. Criteria like Progressive Rollout, Rollback Automation, Health Checks, and Devcontainer Runnable are structurally N/A for any NT8 add-on, including v12 itself (which marks those same criteria as Skipped, not Passed).
Why Build System scores ~57% — and why v12 also skips those same criteria
The v12 monorepo is not purely a NinjaTrader project. It contains a Python orchestration layer (app/app_utils/telemetry.py), a GCP deployment pipeline (.gcp/alerting_policy.json, .github/workflows/staging.yaml), PostHog analytics, Sentry SDK, and a devcontainer. Those are the components that pass criteria like Deployment Frequency, Progressive Rollout, Rollback Automation, Feature Flag Infrastructure, and Dev Container Runnable.
The PTT copier is NT8-only — 5 C# files, no Python layer, no GCP deployment. It deploys by pressing F5 in NinjaTrader and running deploy-sync.ps1 to refresh hard links. There is no staging environment. There is no rollback workflow. There is no Docker container. NT8's Roslyn host compiles the files internally — not via dotnet build, not via CI, not via a deployable artifact.
Criterionv12 passes viaPTT statusResolution
Deployment Frequency staging.yaml → GCP auto-deploy on push to main (Python layer) N/A — NT8 F5 gate is manual by design Structural skip — same reason v12 marks Progressive Rollout N/A
Progressive Rollout N/A in v12 report (same skip) N/A Structural skip
Rollback Automation N/A in v12 report (same skip) N/A Structural skip — git revert + F5 is manual rollback
Monorepo Tooling N/A in v12 report N/A Structural skip
Heavy Dependency Detection N/A in v12 report N/A NT8 DLLs are private local refs, not bundled packages
Feature Flag Infrastructure PostHog in app/app_utils/telemetry.py (Python layer) ⚠ GAP — PTT has no feature flags Infra-B closes this — JSON config file + CopyEngineFlags.cs
Release Notes Automation release-drafter.yml present in repo ✓ Shared — release-drafter.yml covers all files in repo including PTT None — already passes
Unused Dependencies Detection Vulture on Python layer (v12 report says "CHANGED") ⚠ GAP — no equivalent for C# PTT files Infra-D closes this — dead_code_scan.py extended for PTT
Bottom line: The 5 Build System skips are not failures — they are the same N/A marks the v12 report itself assigns to those same criteria for the NT8 component. PTT's actual actionable Build System gap is just two items: Feature Flags (Infra-B) and Dead Code / Unused Dependency scan (Infra-D).

Infrastructure deliverables — execution queue (down-time blocks)
Infra-A — PttLog.cs  ·  Structured file logger HIGHEST VALUE
Lock-free ring-buffer logger (fixed array + Interlocked cursor). Writes [TRACE:NNNNN][MODULE][LEVEL] message entries. Flushes to %NT_USERDATA%\PropTraderTools\ptt_trace.log on OnDestroyed. Also fires Print() to NT output tab in debug builds. Zero NuGet. Zero Sentry. Pure System.IO. 
[TRACE:00001][COPY][INFO]  OrderSubmitted MES SEP26 Limit@4520.25 qty=2
[TRACE:00002][BE][INFO]    BreakEven fired MES SEP26 stop→4521.00+2tks
[TRACE:00003][TRAIL][WARN] TrailBe HWM advanced 4521.00→4523.00
[TRACE:00004][COPY][ERROR] SendCopy null instrument guard hit
Closes: Structured Logging Closes: Error Tracking (error entries in log) Closes: Error-to-Insight Pipeline (log = insight artifact) New file: PttLog.cs Touches: CopyEngine.cs (log calls at key events) NT8-003: volatile long cursor only — no volatile double
Infra-B — CopyEngineFlags.cs  ·  Feature flags from JSON config MEDIUM
JSON config file read once on OnInitialize, cached as static readonly bool fields. If file is absent, all flags default to safe values. Zero behavior change at startup. New features ship defaulting to true; experimental features default false until promoted. 
// PropTraderTools\ptt_flags.json (user-editable, gitignored)
{
  "EnableClickTrader": true,
  "EnableMirrorMode": true,
  "EnableTrailBe": true,
  "EnableAtrSizing": true,
  "EnableStructuredLog": true
}
Closes: Feature Flag Infrastructure New file: CopyEngineFlags.cs New file: ptt_flags.json (gitignored template) Touches: TradeCopierAddOn.cs (load on init)
Infra-C — CopyEngine circuit breaker MEDIUM
_consecutiveCopyErrors int tracked via Interlocked. After 5 consecutive errors in OnOrderUpdate / SendCopy: auto-disable copy engine + fire StatusUpdate("COPY DISABLED — 5 consecutive errors"). Reset on next manual SetEnabled(true). CYC ≤ 2 added to SendCopy. Prevents silent error loops during live trading.
Closes: Circuit Breakers Touches: CopyEngine.cs only (~8 lines added) New [Fact] tests: circuit-break at 5 errors, reset on SetEnabled JS-021: Interlocked only — no lock()
Infra-D — dead_code_scan.py for PropTraderTools LOW EFFORT
The repo-level scripts/dead_code_scan.py does not exist (returns exit:1). Create it scoped to src/PropTraderTools/*.cs — scans for private methods with zero cross-references in the 5-file scope. Wire into CI via dotnet-build.yml as a warning-only step.
Closes: Dead Code Detection Closes: Unused Dependencies Detection (C# scope) New file: scripts/dead_code_scan.py Touches: dotnet-build.yml (add warning step)
Infra-E — XML doc comments on CopyEngine public API LOW EFFORT
Add /// <summary> XML doc comments to all public and internal-visible methods on CopyEngine.cs: SetEnabled, AddRule, DispatchCopy, BreakEven, Flatten, Trim, ArmTrailBe, DisarmTrailBe. Enables docfx or similar to auto-generate an HTML API reference.
Closes: Automated Documentation Generation Touches: CopyEngine.cs only (comments, no logic change) ~40 lines added, CYC unchanged

MISSION -- PTT-INFRA-B1 closes 5 agent-readiness gaps in one block:

  Infra-A: PttLog.cs -- structured ring-buffer file logger
    New file: src/PropTraderTools/PttLog.cs
    Fixed-size string[] ring buffer (capacity=512), int cursor via Interlocked.
    Write() method: formats "[TRACE:{n:D5}][{module}][{level}] {message}", stores in ring.
    Flush() method: writes all ring entries to
      %USERPROFILE%\Documents\NinjaTrader 8\bin\Custom\PropTraderTools\ptt_trace.log
      via StreamWriter append=false (overwrite on each flush -- one log per session).
    FlushAndPrint() variant: also calls NinjaTrader.Core.Globals.Print() for debug visibility.
    Called from CopyEngine at: SetEnabled (INFO), SendCopy success (INFO), SendCopy catch (ERROR),
      BreakEven (INFO), Flatten (INFO), Trim (INFO), ArmTrailBe (INFO), DisarmTrailBe (INFO),
      OnTrailBeAccountUpdate HWM advance (INFO), gate blocks (WARN).
    Flush() called from TradeCopierAddOn.OnDestroyed().
    NT8 constraints: no volatile double. Cursor = volatile int (int is atomic on x86 -- safe without
      Interlocked for reads, use Interlocked.Increment for write). No NuGet. Pure System.IO.
    CYC: Write=2, Flush=2, FlushAndPrint=1. All <= 8.
    Closes: Structured Logging, Error Tracking, Error-to-Insight Pipeline criteria.

  Infra-B: CopyEngineFlags.cs -- feature flags from JSON config
    New file: src/PropTraderTools/CopyEngineFlags.cs
    Static class with static readonly bool fields, loaded once on startup.
    Load() reads %USERPROFILE%\Documents\NinjaTrader 8\bin\Custom\PropTraderTools\ptt_flags.json
      via System.IO.File.ReadAllText + manual string parse (no System.Text.Json -- NT8-004 ban on
      System.Collections.Immutable; verify JSON parser availability before using JsonDocument).
    If file absent: all flags default to true (safe -- enables all stable features).
    Fields: EnableClickTrader, EnableMirrorMode, EnableTrailBe, EnableAtrSizing, EnableStructuredLog.
    Load() called from TradeCopierAddOn.OnInitialize().
    CYC: Load=3 (file-absent guard + per-flag parse). All <= 8.
    New gitignored template: src/PropTraderTools/ptt_flags.json.template (committed).
    Closes: Feature Flag Infrastructure criterion.

  Infra-C: Circuit breaker in CopyEngine
    New field: int _consecutiveCopyErrors (Interlocked-managed, no lock()).
    In SendCopy catch block: Interlocked.Increment(_consecutiveCopyErrors).
      If value reaches 5: call SetEnabled(false) + StatusUpdate("COPY DISABLED -- 5 errors").
    In SendCopy success path: Interlocked.Exchange(ref _consecutiveCopyErrors, 0).
    In SetEnabled(true): Interlocked.Exchange(ref _consecutiveCopyErrors, 0) (reset on re-arm).
    CYC impact: SendCopy gains +1 (one extra branch). Must stay <= 8 -- verify after.
    New [Fact] tests (min 2):
      CircuitBreaker_FiveErrors_DisablesCopyEngine
      CircuitBreaker_Reset_OnSetEnabledTrue
    Closes: Circuit Breakers criterion.

  Infra-D: scripts/dead_code_scan.py -- dead code scanner for PropTraderTools
    New file: scripts/dead_code_scan.py
    Scans src/PropTraderTools/*.cs for private methods (regex: private\s+\w+\s+\w+\().
    For each found private method name, checks whether that name appears anywhere else in the
      5-file scope (cross-reference by simple string search).
    Prints WARNING for any private method with zero references outside its own declaration.
    Exit 0 always (warning-only, never blocks CI).
    Wire as a new step in .github/workflows/dotnet-build.yml:
      name: "Dead code scan (PTT)"
      run: python scripts/dead_code_scan.py
      continue-on-error: true
    Closes: Dead Code Detection + Unused Dependencies Detection criteria.

  Infra-E: XML doc comments on CopyEngine.cs public API
    Add /// summary XML doc comments to these methods in CopyEngine.cs:
      SetEnabled, AddRule, RemoveRule, SetRuleEnabled, DispatchCopy, SendCopy,
      BreakEven, Flatten, Trim, ArmTrailBe, DisarmTrailBe, GetSuggestedQty.
    Comment content: one sentence stating what the method does and its CYC.
    No behavior change. No logic change. CYC unchanged.
    Closes: Automated Documentation Generation criterion.

Architecture Research — Open Investigations
◯ ARCH-BRACKET-03 — CustomOrder arg 12 Sim101 Probe
Question: Does passing a populated new CustomOrder() at arg 12 of Account.CreateOrder() cause NT8 to spawn ATM bracket legs atomically at fill from AddOn context? This is the zero-architecture-change path — if confirmed, the copier gains native ATM brackets with no companion Strategy required.
Background: AtmStrategyCreate() lives on NinjaTrader.NinjaScript.StrategyBase — confirmed from NT8 reflection cache. It is not available on AddOnBase or Account. The only bracket-adjacent API reachable from AddOn context is the CustomOrder object passed as the final arg of Account.CreateOrder().

CustomOrder confirmed members (reflection): AutoBreakEvenPlus · AutoBreakEvenProfitTrigger · AutoTrailSteps · ChaseLimit · IsChaseIfTouched · CalculationMode · OnBehalfOf · IsAutoBreakEvenEnabled · IsAutoTrailEnabled · TickSize · Update · Order · Clone · CopyTo.

No static factory exists. GetCustomOrder() is absent from reflection — only new CustomOrder() is possible.

ATM template XML cross-reference (MES ORB Q6 4SL.xml): The bracket <StopLoss> / <Target> / <Quantity> fields are on AtmStrategy.Brackets[] — NOT on CustomOrder. CustomOrder carries the stop-strategy overlay only (trail, BE, chase). This is the key uncertainty — NT8 may or may not create bracket legs from CustomOrder alone.
Outcome B — Bracket spawns ✓
NT8 honours CustomOrder from AddOn context and spawns stop + target legs at fill. B19 ticket: replace (NinjaTrader.Cbi.CustomOrder)null with BuildCustomOrder(template) helper in SendCopy(). Zero architecture change. AddOn stays AddOn.
Outcome A — No bracket legs ✗
CustomOrder carries trail/BE overlay only. Bracket legs require AtmStrategyCreate() on StrategyBase. Pivot to Option C — PTTFollowerStrategy companion pattern. Architecture change required.
◯ Three test paths — run in order, stop at first success
Path What you pass What to observe
3a new CustomOrder() with trail/BE props set. OnBehalfOf = null. Do stop + target legs appear in Order Flow after fill?
3b new CustomOrder() with OnBehalfOf = "MES ORB Q6 4SL" (exact ATM template name). Does NT8 look up template by name and spawn full bracket?
3c Both 3a + 3b produce no legs. Accept Outcome A. Document. Pivot to Option C (PTTFollowerStrategy).
Probe script — paste into a blank NT8 Strategy, OnStateChange / State.Realtime 
// ARCH-BRACKET-03 Sim101 probe — paste into blank Strategy OnStateChange/State.Realtime
// Run on chart with Sim101 + MES. Check Order Flow + NT8 log after fill.
// This is a read-only probe — cancel the position immediately after testing.

var follower = Account.All.FirstOrDefault(a => a.Name.Contains("Sim101"));
if (follower == null) { /* log: Sim101 not found */ return; }

// ── Path 3a: trail/BE overlay only, no OnBehalfOf ──────────────────
var co = new NinjaTrader.Cbi.CustomOrder();
co.CalculationMode    = NinjaTrader.Cbi.CalculationMode.Ticks;
co.AutoBreakEvenPlus           = 1;
co.AutoBreakEvenProfitTrigger  = 8;
co.IsAutoBreakEvenEnabled      = true;
co.ChaseLimit                  = 1;
co.IsChaseIfTouched            = true;
// co.OnBehalfOf = "MES ORB Q6 4SL";  // ← uncomment for Path 3b

follower.CreateOrder(
    Instrument,                           // current chart instrument
    OrderAction.Buy,
    OrderType.Market,
    OrderEntry.Manual,
    TimeInForce.Day,
    1,                                    // qty: 1 contract
    0,                                    // limitPrice: 0 for market
    0,                                    // stopPrice: 0
    null,                                 // oconame
    "PTT-ARCH-BRACKET-03-TEST",           // signal name (PTT- prefix)
    DateTime.Now.AddDays(1),             // gtd
    co                                    // ← populated CustomOrder (not null)
);
What to observe after the order fills:
  1. Order Flow — does a stop order appear automatically?
  2. Order Flow — does a target order appear automatically?
  3. Position panel — does it show "ATM active" or an ATM strategy ID?
  4. Does NT8 throw a runtime exception on the CreateOrder call? (check NT8 log)
  5. NT8 Control Center → Log — copy the exact log text from the fill event.
First — check CustomOrder constructor args (30 seconds):
In NinjaScript Editor → new blank Indicator → type var co = new NinjaTrader.Cbi.CustomOrder( and wait for Intellisense. If the constructor takes stopTicks / targetTicks params, those are the bracket leg values — pass them directly. If it takes zero params (default constructor only), bracket legs are NOT in CustomOrder and Outcome A is likely. Report the Intellisense signature back before running the full probe.

◯ Block 24  ·  PTT-COPIER-B24  ·  BE Independence: Leader Wiring Fix + AllAccounts Decoupling
B24 objective: fix the two root causes that make the BE button non-functional for solo use (no copy rule required). Discovered during B23 post-commit E2E testing. Both defects are independent — 2 lanes run in parallel. Lane A touches only TradeCopierAddOn.cs. Lane B touches CopyEngine.cs + TradeCopierPanel.cs + CopyEngineTests.cs. No shared edit sites — parallel execution safe.

[Fact] baseline entering B24: 126  ·  Target after B24: 128 (Lane A +0, Lane B +2)
B24 DEFECT REGISTER
ID P Root Cause Status
DW-B24-LEADER-CASTNULL-01 P0 WireLeaderAccount cast fails at inject time — panel shows "No leader" permanently even with Sim101 visually selected in ChartTrader.
Confirmed (Director screenshot, B24 session): ChartTrader Account ComboBox clearly shows Sim101. Status bar reads "No leader — select account in ChartTrader". Every panel button (BE, Trim, Flatten, Cancel, Tighten) is dead.
Root cause: WireLeaderAccount (TradeCopierAddOn.cs) does: var current = accountCombo.SelectedItem as Account; At NT8 inject time the WPF ComboBox SelectedItem is a framework placeholder object — not yet a materialised NinjaTrader.Cbi.Account. The cast returns null. SetLeaderAccount is never called. The SelectionChanged subscription IS wired, so the panel self-corrects the first time the user manually changes the account dropdown — but never on a cold start.
Fix (Lane A): After the failed cast, fall back to Account.All.FirstOrDefault(a => string.Equals(a.Name, accountCombo.Text, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)). ComboBox.Text is reliably populated from the display string before SelectedItem resolves. OrdinalIgnoreCase prevents silent failure on NT8 installs where account display alias casing differs. One-time cost at inject (linear scan of Account.All, typically 2–5 accounts). CYC 4 → 6. 		✓ CLOSED — Lane A
DW-B23-BE-ALLACCOUNTS-01 P0 BreakEven silently no-ops for solo users — routes through AllAccounts → FindRule → yields nothing when no copy rule is registered.
Confirmed (source audit, B24 session): BreakEven(Instrument, int) (CopyEngine.cs:1176) calls AllAccounts(instrument) which calls FindRule(instrument). If no rule exists, AllAccounts yields nothing. MoveStopToBreakEven is never called. No exception. No log entry. Stop never moves.
Affects: BE button, BE+/- spinners, keyboard B shortcut, click-trader BE button — all 5 BE call sites in TradeCopierPanel.
Also affects (B25 scope): Trim, Flatten, Cancel, TightenStop — all route through AllAccounts identically. Deferred to B25 to maintain B24 focus.
Root cause — architecture: The panel holds _leaderAccount but never passes it to engine BE calls. The engine has no direct-account path — AllAccounts is the only route. The copier and the BE button were designed to be modular (sold separately) but share the same account-resolution path.
Fix (Lane B): Add internal void BreakEven(Account leader, Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks) overload that calls MoveStopToBreakEven(leader, instrument, bufferTicks) directly for the leader (no rule required), then also fans out to AllAccounts(instrument) followers if a rule exists — deduplicating leader. Update all 5 BE panel call sites to pass _leaderAccount. Update OnPendingBeAccountUpdate line 1396 to pass local acc. Existing BreakEven(Instrument, int) overload unchanged (TrailBe uses it). CYC of new overload = 3. Add StatusUpdate diagnostic when no rule and no leader (C's recommendation — zero CYC cost, critical for live debugging). 		✓ CLOSED — Lane B

  • Lane B — new overload param order: BreakEven(Account leader, Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks). Account first — matches MoveStopToBreakEven(Account, Instrument, int) convention. NOT BreakEven(Instrument, Account, int).
  • Lane B — leader always first, followers deduplicated: New overload calls MoveStopToBreakEven(leader, ...) directly (no rule required). Then iterates AllAccounts(instrument) for followers, skipping any that == leader to avoid double-move. If leader == null: returns early (does NOT fall through to old overload — Judge A's fallback is dangerous; a null leader is a misconfiguration, not a "try rule-based path" scenario).
  • Lane B — StatusUpdate diagnostic: When leader == null, fire StatusUpdate?.Invoke("PTT-BE: leader null — BE skipped"); before returning. Zero CYC cost. Critical for live debugging (Judge C: "a user clicking BE under live conditions, seeing no response, and not knowing why" is the worst operational scenario).

LANE B — NEW OVERLOAD SPECIFICATION
// CopyEngine.cs — new overload. CYC=3.
// JS-021: no lock. JS-002: null leader → status + early return (not silent skip).
// Called from panel when _leaderAccount is known. Existing BreakEven(Instrument, int) retained for TrailBe.
internal void BreakEven(Account leader, Instrument instrument, int bufferTicks)
{
    if (leader == null)                                          // (1) null guard
    {
        StatusUpdate?.Invoke("PTT-BE: leader null -- BE skipped");
        return;
    }
    MoveStopToBreakEven(leader, instrument, bufferTicks);        // leader direct -- no rule needed
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))              // (2) follower fan-out (may yield nothing)
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;                             // (3) skip duplicate
        MoveStopToBreakEven(acc, instrument, bufferTicks);
    }
}

TradeCopierPanel.cs:859OnBeConnectedBreakEven(_instrument, _beBuffer)BreakEven(_leaderAccount, _instrument, _beBuffer)
TradeCopierPanel.cs:782OnBeUpBreakEven(_instrument, _beBuffer)BreakEven(_leaderAccount, _instrument, _beBuffer)
TradeCopierPanel.cs:791OnBeDownBreakEven(_instrument, _beBuffer)BreakEven(_leaderAccount, _instrument, _beBuffer)
TradeCopierPanel.cs:1299OnBreakEvenBreakEven(_instrument, ticks)BreakEven(_leaderAccount, _instrument, ticks)

B25 DEFECT REGISTER
ID P Root Cause Status
DW-B25-01
DW-B24-BE-STOPLIMIT-01		 P0 BE button is a total no-op when any ATM strategy is active — gate 4 in MoveStopToBreakEven silently skips all StopLimit orders.
Confirmed (live test + source audit, B24 post-commit session): ATM strategy "MES RMA 4SL 12Q" places bracket stops as OrderType.StopLimit with name format "12s Buy STP". MoveStopToBreakEven (CopyEngine.cs:1152) gate 4 reads:
if (order.OrderType != OrderType.StopMarket) continue;
This skips 100% of ATM bracket stops. The iteration continues but no acc.Change() is ever called. No exception. No log. Stop never moves.
Precedent confirmed: TightenStop at CopyEngine.cs:1234-1235 ALREADY uses the two-type pattern:
if (order.OrderType != OrderType.StopMarket && order.OrderType != OrderType.StopLimit) continue;
acc.Change() on StopLimit confirmed safe (same path as TightenStop in production). LimitPrice does not need to be set — NT8 recalculates from original offset. CYC of MoveStopToBreakEven stays 6 (one branch replaced by a two-condition OR — branch-count unchanged).
Secondary hardening — IsStopLeg: IsStopLeg at CopyEngine.cs:1092 uses order.FromEntrySignal != null || order.Name.StartsWith("Stop"). ATM bracket stop names use format "12s Buy STP" — no "Stop" prefix, no FromEntrySignal. This means the stop passes gate 4 (after fix) but fails gate 6 (IsStopLeg). Fix: add || (order.Name != null && order.Name.EndsWith("STP", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) fallback. CYC 2→3. Best-of-N confirmed this is necessary; without it the fix is incomplete for ATM orders. 		✓ CLOSED — commit e8045854
DW-B25-02 P0 Multi-chart singleton BE state corruption — two panels on different accounts share one volatile int state machine.
Observed (live test, B24 post-commit session): Sim101 + Sim102 open simultaneously. One panel's DisarmPendingBe() corrupts the other's armed state. Sim102 stuck on "BE Live". Sim101 cycling between "BE+1" and "BE Armed" after Sim102's stop was moved.
Root cause: _pendingBeState and _trailBeState are single volatile int fields on CopyEngine.Instance (singleton). Every TradeCopierPanel writes to the same field. The arm/disarm FSM has no concept of which account it is operating on.
Fix (Lane B): Replace _pendingBeState / _trailBeState with ConcurrentDictionary<string, int> keyed by account.Name. ArmPendingBe / DisarmPendingBe / ArmTrailBe / DisarmTrailBe take an Account parameter. OnPendingBeAccountUpdate / OnTrailBeAccountUpdate resolve their slot by account name. JS-021 satisfied: ConcurrentDictionary is lock-free. NT8 compiler: no ImmutableDictionary — use ConcurrentDictionary (NT8-004). CYC of each arm/disarm method stays ≤3. No new [Fact] required — existing FSM arm/disarm tests must be updated to pass account parameter. 		✓ CLOSED — commit e8045854

Edit 3 — IsStopLeg hardening (CopyEngine.cs ~L1090-1093)
// BEFORE (CYC=2):
private bool IsStopLeg(Order order)
{
    return order.FromEntrySignal != null || (order.Name != null && order.Name.StartsWith("Stop"));
}

// AFTER (CYC=3 — adds STP suffix fallback for ATM bracket names like "12s Buy STP"):
// B25 T1 -- DW-B25-01: ATM bracket stops use name format "12s Buy STP".
// FromEntrySignal is null for ATM orders. No "Stop" prefix. STP suffix is the only discriminator.
// CYC: 2 + 1 (STP clause) = 3. OrdinalIgnoreCase: consistent with WireLeaderAccount (B24 Lane A).
private bool IsStopLeg(Order order)
{
    return order.FromEntrySignal != null
        || (order.Name != null && order.Name.StartsWith("Stop"))
        || (order.Name != null && order.Name.EndsWith("STP", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
}

NT8 BE BUTTON — END-TO-END TEST PROCEDURE (after B25 Lane A applied)
Prerequisites: Open Sim101, open a long MES position (any size), ATM strategy active (e.g. "MES RMA 4SL 12Q"). Trade Copier panel visible in ChartTrader. No copy rule required (solo use — B24 fix).
  1. Panel state check before clicking: Verify the status bar shows the account name (not "No leader — select account in ChartTrader"). If "No leader" appears: the B24 Lane A fix is not applied. Stop — do not proceed. Confirm instrument name is shown in the panel header. Confirm BE buffer spinner shows a number (default 2).
  2. The 3 BE button states:
    • Idle — grey/default. No pending arm. Clicking transitions to Armed.
    • Armed ("BE+N") — amber/yellow highlight. Engine is watching the position. Will auto-fire when price reaches entry + N ticks above (long) or entry - N ticks below (short).
    • Connected / BE Live — green highlight. BE has fired. Stop is at (or moving toward) BE level. ArmTrailBe is active — stop trails upward as price continues.
  3. Buffer spinner — what value to use for MES: MES tick size = 0.25 index points. Buffer = 1 means stop will be placed at entry + 0.25 (1 tick above entry for a long). Buffer = 0 means stop placed exactly at entry (true break-even). Buffer = 2 is the default (stop 0.50 above entry). Recommended for live test: leave at 2 (safe: stop is 2 ticks above entry, not at entry).
  4. Armed auto-fire trigger (long MES position): After clicking BE (state = Armed), watch the DOM/price ladder. Auto-fire triggers when bid >= entry + buffer * 0.25 (for a long). Example: entry = 5500.00, buffer = 2 → auto-fires when bid ≥ 5500.50. Visually: the price ladder crosses the armed trigger level, the BE button turns green.
  5. What "BE Live" (Connected state) means: The stop has been moved to entry + buffer * tickSize. ArmTrailBe is now active. TrailBe means: every tick price moves up (for a long), the stop is pushed up by acc.Change() to maintain the buffer distance. The stop never moves down — only up. This is a ratchet trail. Visual confirmation: check the Orders tab — the stop order's price should be above your average entry.
  6. Confirm the stop actually moved: Positions tab → expand the MES position → check "Stop" price against your entry. Should be entry + (buffer × 0.25). E.g. entry 5500.00, buffer 2 → stop at 5500.50. Orders tab → Working orders → find the ATM bracket stop (name ends "STP") → StopPrice column should match. NT8 status bar / Output window: look for "Sim101: BE moved to 5500.50" from StatusUpdate. Also look for: "Sim101: MoveStopToBreakEven: StopLimit bracket stop -> acc.Change" (new B25 diagnostic log).
  7. If Armed never fires (price reaches target but stop doesn't move): Check NT8 Output window for any PTT-BE error: lines. If DW-B25-01 fix is NOT applied: gate 4 will still silently skip StopLimit. The diagnostic line will NOT appear (it's inside the try block, only reached if gate passes). Workaround before B25: manually move the stop in the Orders tab. Post-B25: if still not firing, check IsStopLeg — ATM order name should end "STP" after IsStopLeg hardening.
  8. If BE Live but stop is at wrong price: Check buffer value in spinner — was it set to 0 accidentally? (stop at entry = 0 buffer). Check whether pos.AveragePrice returned the correct entry (multiple fills = weighted average). Check NT8 Output window for: "Sim101: BE moved to XXXX" — the logged price is the exact value set. If the logged price is correct but the order shows a different price: NT8 may have rejected acc.Change() silently (rare on sim accounts; more common with Rithmic in live).
  9. Reset to Idle: Click the Armed (amber) button once to cancel the arm and return to Idle. DisarmPendingBe() is called — state → Idle. The button returns to grey. If in Connected (BE Live) state: there is no disarm for the trail (by design — the stop is already moved, ratchet is protecting the position). Close the position normally to end the session.
  10. Solo-use scenario after B24 vs before B24: Before B24: Panel shows "No leader" (WireLeaderAccount cast fails at inject time). All buttons are dead. No account is known. BE button click is a no-op even if ATM is active. After B24 + before B25: Panel correctly shows account name. BE click works (leader is set, 3-arg overload is used). But the stop STILL does not move for ATM strategies because gate 4 filters StopLimit orders. After B25 Lane A: Gate 4 accepts StopLimit. IsStopLeg hardened for "STP" suffix. Stop moves correctly for ATM bracket strategies. Solo use is fully functional.
DW-B25-02 — MULTI-CHART ISOLATION LIVE TEST PROCEDURE
What this test confirms: Two TradeCopierPanel instances (Sim101 + Sim102) running simultaneously can each arm/fire/trail BE independently without cross-contaminating each other's state machine. Before B25 Lane B, arming Sim102 would corrupt Sim101's state (shared singleton volatile int). After B25 Lane B, each account has its own slot in _pendingBeStates / _trailBeStates.
Prerequisites:
  • Two separate NT8 charts open — Chart A on Sim101, Chart B on Sim102.
  • Both charts have a TradeCopierPanel visible in ChartTrader.
  • Both panels show their respective account names (not "No leader").
  • Open a long MES position on Sim101 with an ATM strategy active.
  • Open a long MES position on Sim102 with an ATM strategy active (separate entry price recommended — e.g. fill Sim102 a few ticks later so entry prices differ).
  1. Arm Sim101 only — verify isolation: On Chart A (Sim101): click the BE button once → state = Armed (amber). On Chart B (Sim102): verify the BE button is still Idle (grey, unchanged). ✅ PASS: Sim102 Idle. ❌ FAIL: Sim102 also shows Armed — singleton still shared.
  2. Arm Sim102 — verify both can be armed simultaneously: On Chart B (Sim102): click the BE button → state = Armed. Both panels now show Armed (amber) at the same time. ✅ PASS: both independently Armed. ❌ FAIL: arming Sim102 disarmed or changed Sim101.
  3. Disarm Sim102 — verify Sim101 survives: On Chart B (Sim102): click the Armed button once → DisarmPendingBe(Sim102) fires → state → Idle. On Chart A (Sim101): verify Sim101 is still Armed (amber). ✅ PASS: Sim101 unaffected. ❌ FAIL: Sim101 drops to Idle when Sim102 is disarmed — classic singleton bug.
  4. Fire BE on Sim101 — verify Sim102 stays Idle: With Sim101 still Armed: allow price to reach Sim101's auto-fire threshold (bid ≥ entry + buffer × 0.25). Alternatively: manually move price past the threshold in the DOM simulator. Observe: Sim101 transitions to BE Live (green). Sim102 remains Idle (grey). ✅ PASS: independent fire, Sim102 unaffected. ❌ FAIL: Sim102 also flips to BE Live.
  5. Confirm stop moved on Sim101 only: NT8 Orders tab → Working orders: Sim101's ATM bracket stop (name ends "STP") should have moved to entry + buffer × 0.25. Sim102's stop should be unchanged (at its original ATM placement). NT8 Output window: look for "Sim101: MoveStopToBreakEven: StopLimit bracket stop → acc.Change". There should be no corresponding line for Sim102 at this point. ✅ PASS: only Sim101 stop moved. ❌ FAIL: Sim102 stop also moved, or Output shows Sim102 log line.
  6. Arm and fire Sim102 independently: Click BE on Chart B (Sim102) → Armed → allow price to reach fire threshold on Sim102. Observe: Sim102 transitions to BE Live. Sim101 (already in BE Live) is unaffected. Both panels are in BE Live simultaneously, each tracking their own account's stop. ✅ PASS: independent fires, no cross-contamination. ❌ FAIL: any state change on one panel affects the other.
  7. Stress test — rapid arm/disarm alternation: On Chart A: arm Sim101. On Chart B: arm Sim102. On Chart A: disarm Sim101. On Chart B: disarm Sim102. Repeat 3–4 times at speed. Observe: both panels track their own state independently throughout. ✅ PASS: no state bleed after rapid alternation. ❌ FAIL: any panel ends in wrong state.
  8. Expected NT8 Output window diagnostic lines: // When Sim101 BE fires: "Sim101: MoveStopToBreakEven: StopLimit bracket stop -> acc.Change" "Sim101: BE moved to [price]" // When Sim102 BE fires (independently): "Sim102: MoveStopToBreakEven: StopLimit bracket stop -> acc.Change" "Sim102: BE moved to [price]" // Each line prefixed by its own account name — no mixing.
Note: If only one sim account is available, the DW-B25-02 test can be deferred or approximated by opening two chart windows on the same Sim101 account. The real isolation test requires two distinct accounts.

⚠ Block 27  ·  PTT-COPIER-B27  ·  BE Singleton Fields — Stop Never Moves — OPEN
B27 objective: one root-cause defect found after Director confirmed DW-B26-02 is fixed (panels now isolated) but stop loss still does not move when price passes the BE level.
DW-B27-01 — _pendingBeAccount, _pendingBeInstrument, _pendingBeBufferTicks and their trail equivalents are still singleton plain fields on CopyEngine — one slot shared by all accounts. The _pendingBeStates dictionary (B25-02 fix) tracks whether each account is armed, but the data those callbacks read is still a single shared reference. With two panels armed sequentially the second arm overwrites the first. With even one panel, the UI-thread write and the NT8 background-thread read have no memory barrier.
B27 DEFECT REGISTER
ID P Root Cause & Fix Status
DW-B27-01 P0 BE pending/trail state fields are singleton — second arm overwrites first; background thread has no memory barrier on reads.

Root cause (source audit, 2026-07-16):
_pendingBeStates (line 100) is correctly a ConcurrentDictionary<string,int> keyed by account name (B25-02 fix). ✅
But the three companion fields that OnPendingBeAccountUpdate actually reads are still single shared slots:
  • L102: private Account _pendingBeAccount = null — plain reference, no barrier
  • L103: private Instrument _pendingBeInstrument = null — plain reference, no barrier
  • L101: private volatile int _pendingBeBufferTicks = 2 — volatile int (barrier present but still one slot)
Same problem in the trail path (L111-114): _trailBeAccount, _trailBeInstrument, _trailBeBufferTicks — all singleton.

Failure scenario (two panels):
1. Panel Sim101 arms → _pendingBeAccount = Sim101, _pendingBeInstrument = MES
2. Panel Sim102 arms → _pendingBeAccount = Sim102 (overwrites), _pendingBeInstrument = MES
3. Sim101 price crosses trigger → OnPendingBeAccountUpdate runs with acc = Sim101 (captured) but reads _pendingBeInstrument which may now be null (DisarmPendingBe on Sim102 nulled it) → gate 4 exits, stop never moves.

Failure scenario (one panel, memory ordering):
ArmPendingBe runs on the UI thread and writes _pendingBeInstrument = instr then _pendingBeStates[acc.Name] = 1.
OnPendingBeAccountUpdate runs on the NT8 account background thread and reads _pendingBeInstrument without any barrier. The comment at L102-103 says "single-writer UI thread" — but the reader is the background thread. Without volatile or an Interlocked fence, the background thread can observe a stale null. The dict write at L1307 provides a release fence for the dict value only, not for the plain reference fields written before it.

Fix (Lane A) — per-account slot struct:
Replace the three singleton pending-BE fields and the three singleton trail-BE fields with two ConcurrentDictionarys of a small value struct:
// CopyEngine.cs — replace L101-103
private struct PendingBeSlot
{
    internal readonly Account    Account;
    internal readonly Instrument Instrument;
    internal readonly int        BufferTicks;
    internal PendingBeSlot(Account a, Instrument i, int b)
    { Account = a; Instrument = i; BufferTicks = b; }
}
// Replace _pendingBeStates + 3 singleton fields with one dict:
private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, PendingBeSlot> _pendingBeSlots
    = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, PendingBeSlot>();

// Same pattern for trail:
private struct TrailBeSlot
{
    internal readonly Account    Account;
    internal readonly Instrument Instrument;
    internal readonly int        BufferTicks;
    internal          long       LastPnlBits;  // Interlocked CAS
    internal TrailBeSlot(Account a, Instrument i, int b, long pnl)
    { Account = a; Instrument = i; BufferTicks = b; LastPnlBits = pnl; }
}
private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, TrailBeSlot> _trailBeSlots
    = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, TrailBeSlot>();
ArmPendingBe becomes a single dict write: _pendingBeSlots[masterAcc.Name] = new PendingBeSlot(masterAcc, instr, bufferTicks);
OnPendingBeAccountUpdate reads from the dict: if (!_pendingBeSlots.TryRemove(acc.Name, out var slot)) return; then uses slot.Instrument, slot.BufferTicks — no stale-read possible.
DisarmPendingBe becomes: _pendingBeSlots.TryRemove(leader.Name, out _);
The IsPendingBeArmed helper is replaced by _pendingBeSlots.ContainsKey(acc.Name).

NT8 constraint: NT8 uses .NET Framework 4.8. struct with readonly fields compiles fine (NT8-001 is init setters — not readonly). No ImmutableDictionary (NT8-004 banned) — using plain struct value in ConcurrentDictionary is safe.

CYC impact:
ArmPendingBe: CYC 4→3 (flat skip guard removed — dict write replaces multi-field writes).
OnPendingBeAccountUpdate: CYC 8→7 (TryRemove replaces two-step IsPendingBeArmed + separate TryRemove at L1454).
IsPendingBeArmed helper: deleted (replaced by ContainsKey).
IsTrailBeArmed helper: deleted (replaced by ContainsKey).
Net: simpler, not more complex.

[Fact] additions required:
T_B27_01_ArmTwoPanels_SecondArmDoesNotNullFirstInstrument — arm Sim101, arm Sim102, trigger Sim101 → instrument not null.
T_B27_02_DisarmOneAccount_DoesNotAffectOther — arm both, disarm Sim102, Sim101 slot still present.
Baseline 133 → target 135. 		OPEN
B27 ARCHITECTURE DECISION — LOCKED
  • Consolidate all pending-BE state into one dict per account. Replace _pendingBeStates (int dict) + _pendingBeAccount + _pendingBeInstrument + _pendingBeBufferTicks with a single ConcurrentDictionary<string, PendingBeSlot> _pendingBeSlots. Presence in dict = armed; absence = disarmed. No separate state int needed.
  • Same consolidation for trail-BE fields. Replace _trailBeStates + _trailBeAccount + _trailBeInstrument + _trailBeBufferTicks + _trailBeLastPnl with ConcurrentDictionary<string, TrailBeSlot> _trailBeSlots. TrailBeSlot.LastPnlBits mutated via Interlocked.CompareExchange on the value inside the dict (use AddOrUpdate or read-modify-write pattern).
  • One lane only. Both pending and trail slots live in CopyEngine.cs. No change to TradeCopierPanel.cs — all call sites already pass the Account parameter.
  • JS-021 satisfied. ConcurrentDictionary is lock-free. No lock() introduced. Interlocked.CompareExchange on the PnL bits satisfies JS-021.
  • NT8-004 satisfied. Plain struct values in ConcurrentDictionary are fine. ImmutableDictionary is not used.

✓ Block 28  ·  PTT-COPIER-B28  ·  BE Stop Never Moves + All Action Buttons Silent — CLOSED
B28 objective: two defects discovered during live test of B27 on 2026-07-16 (Director: Sim101 LONG + Sim102 SHORT, MES SEP26, B27 Lane A code deployed, F5 GREEN, [Fact]=135).
DW-B28-01 — BE button changes state to "Live" but acc.Change() is never called. Root cause: OnPendingBeAccountUpdate gate (5) blocks on instr.MarketData.Last.Price returning 0.0 for a sim account, silently returning before BreakEven() is reached. PendingBeFired is NEVER invoked (it is unreachable past gate 5), yet the button still shows "BE Live" — revealing a second independent path that transitions button state (the direct BreakEven call in OnBeConnected itself is also never reached because PendingBeFired never fires).
REVISED ROOT CAUSE THEORY (2026-07-16 diagnosis): The button state change to "Live" means OnBeConnected WAS invoked (PendingBeFired DID fire). This proves gate (5) PASSED (last price was valid). The stop still did not move. The most likely cause is BreakEven(removed.Account, removed.Instrument, removed.BufferTicks) at L1448 called MoveStopToBreakEven, which iterated acc.Orders, but no stop order passed all 7 gates because the ATM stops are named "Stop1"/"Stop2"/"Stop3"/"Stop4" and are type StopMarket — these should pass. The actual blocker is AllAccounts(instrument) inside BreakEven(Account,Instrument,int) which calls FindRule(instrument) — if no rule exists (COPY OFF and Apply Rule never clicked), the follower fan-out is empty. However, the leader's stop IS moved directly via MoveStopToBreakEven(leader, instrument, bufferTicks) before the loop. The leader stop SHOULD have moved. The most likely remaining cause: IsStopAlreadyAtBe() guard or acc.Change() silently throwing an exception that is swallowed. StatusUpdate messages would have revealed which gate blocked but the panel status bar was not monitored in real time.
DW-B28-02 — Cancel, Trim, and Flatten buttons are all silent no-ops. Root cause: AllAccounts(instrument) returns an empty enumerable when FindRule(instrument) finds no matching rule in _rules. Because no rule has been applied ("Apply Rule" not clicked, COPY OFF), all three engine methods iterate zero accounts and submit no orders. No error or warning is emitted.

DW-B28-01 P0 BE fires event and button changes state but stop loss never moves.

Confirmed evidence (2026-07-16 log):
• NT8 Grid log: zero Change submitted events for any stop order across the entire session.
• Button state: Armed → Live (confirms PendingBeFired fired → OnBeConnected was called).
• BreakEven(removed.Account, removed.Instrument, removed.BufferTicks) at L1448 was called.
• MoveStopToBreakEven was called for the leader account.
• All 4 ATM stops: type=StopMarket, state=Working, names=Stop1/Stop2/Stop3/Stop4 — all pass gates 4/5/6.

Most likely blocking gate (requires re-test with status bar visible):
Gate A — IsStopAlreadyAtBe(order, newStop, isLong) at L1186 returns true for all 4 stops, causing silent skips. This would happen if newStop was already equal to or past the existing stop price. With entry 7566.25, buffer=2, tickSize=0.25: newStop = 7566.25 + 0.50 = 7566.75. Existing stop = 7563.25. 7566.75 > 7563.25 for a long → stop should NOT be skipped. Unlikely cause.
Gate B — acc.Change(new Order[] { order }) throws an exception that is swallowed by try/catch at L1200. Exception message written to StatusUpdate → panel status bar label only. Director did not monitor the status bar label during test. Most likely cause — acc.Change() rejected by NT8 for a sim account.
Gate C — BreakEven(removed.Account, ...) is called, but removed.Account is null. The guard at L1218 fires: StatusUpdate?.Invoke("PTT-BE: leader null") and returns. Possible if DisarmPendingBe ran before TryRemove completed, nulling the slot. Possible — requires status bar capture to confirm.

Required next test (mandatory before any code change):
Re-run the same test with the PTT panel status bar (bottom label) visible on screen and captured in a screenshot. The StatusUpdate messages from MoveStopToBreakEven will reveal exactly which gate fired:
  • "Sim101: BE moved to 7566.75" → stop DID move (check Orders tab again)
  • "PTT-BE error: <msg>" → acc.Change() exception — copy the message for B28 triage
  • "PTT-BE: leader null -- BE skipped" → removed.Account was null
  • No message after BE Live → BreakEven was not called (slot issue) OR MoveStopToBreakEven found no orders
Proposed fix (pending status bar confirmation):
If Gate B (acc.Change exception): add a StatusUpdate before AND inside the catch, so the exception is always visible. The fix is diagnostic first, repair second.
If Gate C (removed.Account null): add null guard before L1448 call with StatusUpdate on skip. 		CLOSED — acc.Change() replaced with cancel+replace (B29 DW-B29-02). F5 GREEN 2026-07-16.
DW-B28-02 P1 Cancel, Trim, and Flatten buttons are silent no-ops when no rule is applied.

Root cause (HIGH confidence — code audit 2026-07-16):
Trim(Instrument), Flatten(Instrument), and CancelPendingEntries(Instrument) all route through AllAccounts(instrument) → FindRule(instrument).
FindRule returns null when _rules is empty or has no entry matching instrument.FullName.
AllAccounts immediately yield break on null rule → the foreach loop iterates zero accounts.
No order is submitted, no error is raised, no StatusUpdate message fires.

Reproduction (Director session 2026-07-16):
Both panels showed "COPY OFF". "Apply Rule" had NOT been clicked. _rules was empty. Every button click executed zero account iterations silently.

Proposed fix — two options:
Option A (preferred — direct account path for UI buttons): Add a leader-account overload to Trim, Flatten, and CancelPendingEntries that accepts Account leader as the first argument. The panel passes _leaderAccount. The engine operates on the leader directly without requiring a rule entry. This mirrors the BreakEven(Account leader, Instrument, int) pattern already established at CopyEngine.cs:1216.
Panel call sites change from: _engine.Trim(_instrument) to _engine.Trim(_leaderAccount, _instrument).
Engine new overloads: operate on leader directly, then optionally fan-out via AllAccounts for followers (if rule exists).
Option B (guard + warning): Add a guard at the top of Trim/Flatten/Cancel: if AllAccounts returns empty, emit StatusUpdate("No rule applied — apply rule first"). This surfaces the problem immediately without changing the architecture. Does NOT fix the underlying gap (buttons still don't act on leader-only sessions).

Director decision required: Option A or B?
Option A requires 3 new engine overloads + 3 panel call site changes = ~30 lines.
Option B requires 3 guard additions = ~9 lines. Simpler but incomplete. 		CLOSED — leader-direct overloads shipped in B28 Lane B. F5 GREEN 2026-07-16.
B28 ARCHITECTURE DECISIONS — LOCKED (Director: proceed)
  • DW-B28-01 (Lane A) — diagnostic hardening: Add one StatusUpdate line immediately before acc.Change() in MoveStopToBreakEven so any future exception is immediately visible in the panel status bar. This makes the "attempting" message fire even if acc.Change throws and is swallowed. 1-line add inside the existing try block. CYC unchanged. No new [Fact] required.
  • DW-B28-02 (Lane B) — Option A: leader-account overloads: Add Trim(Account leader, Instrument), Flatten(Account leader, Instrument), and CancelPendingEntries(Account leader, Instrument) to CopyEngine.cs. Each operates on leader directly, then fans out via AllAccounts for followers (skipping leader) — identical to BreakEven(Account,Instrument,int) at L1216. Extract inner loop bodies to private helpers (TrimOneAccount, FlattenOneAccount, CancelOneAccount) to avoid duplicating CreateOrder/Cancel logic. Update 3 panel call sites to pass _leaderAccount as first arg.
  • Also update 4-arg Trim/Flatten overloads: Trim(Account, Instrument, int, double, double) and Flatten(Account, Instrument, int, double, double) follow same leader-direct + follower fan-out pattern. Panel passes _leaderAccount to these too.
  • JS-021 satisfied: No lock(). All new methods are UI-thread initiated synchronous calls.
  • CYC impact: New overloads CYC=4 each. Helper extractions CYC=3 each. All ≤8 per Jane Street standard.
  • [Fact] target: 135 → 138. T_B28_01: Trim(Account,Instrument) overload exists (reflection). T_B28_02: Flatten(Account,Instrument) overload exists (reflection). T_B28_03: CancelPendingEntries(Account,Instrument) overload exists (reflection). Pattern: same as T_B26_01 (GetMethod + BindingFlags.NonPublic|Instance + null-arg smoke test).

Step 2 — CopyEngine.cs: add three leader-account overloads (after existing Flatten at ~L928):

// B28 T2 -- Trim(Account,Instrument): leader-direct + follower fan-out.
// CYC=4: null(1), leader direct, foreach(2), leader skip(3), TrimOneAccount(4).
// JS-021: no lock. Mirrors BreakEven(Account,Instrument,int) at L1216.
internal void Trim(Account leader, Instrument instrument)
{
    if (leader == null) { Trim(instrument); return; }
    TrimOneAccount(leader, instrument);
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;
        TrimOneAccount(acc, instrument);
    }
}

// B28 T2 -- Flatten(Account,Instrument): same pattern. CYC=4.
internal void Flatten(Account leader, Instrument instrument)
{
    if (leader == null) { Flatten(instrument); return; }
    FlattenOneAccount(leader, instrument);
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;
        FlattenOneAccount(acc, instrument);
    }
}

// B28 T2 -- CancelPendingEntries(Account,Instrument): same pattern. CYC=4.
internal void CancelPendingEntries(Account leader, Instrument instrument)
{
    if (leader == null) { CancelPendingEntries(instrument); return; }
    CancelOneAccount(leader, instrument);
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;
        CancelOneAccount(acc, instrument);
    }
}

// B28 T2 -- Trim(Account,Instrument,int,double,double): leader-direct 4-arg.
// CYC=5: null(1), ask/bid guard(2), leader direct, foreach(3), leader skip(4), TrimOneAccount(5).
internal void Trim(Account leader, Instrument instrument, int exitBuffer, double ask, double bid)
{
    if (leader == null) { Trim(instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid); return; }
    if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || exitBuffer == 0) { Trim(leader, instrument); return; }
    TrimOneAccountLimit(leader, instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid);
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;
        TrimOneAccountLimit(acc, instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid);
    }
}

// B28 T2 -- Flatten(Account,Instrument,int,double,double): same. CYC=5.
internal void Flatten(Account leader, Instrument instrument, int exitBuffer, double ask, double bid)
{
    if (leader == null) { Flatten(instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid); return; }
    if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || exitBuffer == 0) { Flatten(leader, instrument); return; }
    FlattenOneAccountLimit(leader, instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid);
    foreach (var acc in AllAccounts(instrument))
    {
        if (acc == leader) continue;
        FlattenOneAccountLimit(acc, instrument, exitBuffer, ask, bid);
    }
}

Step 3 — TradeCopierPanel.cs: update 3 click handlers to pass _leaderAccount:

// OnTrimClick (~L739) — BEFORE:
if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || _trimBuffer == 0)
    _engine.Trim(_instrument);
else
    _engine.Trim(_instrument, _trimBuffer, ask, bid);

// AFTER:
if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || _trimBuffer == 0)
    _engine.Trim(_leaderAccount, _instrument);
else
    _engine.Trim(_leaderAccount, _instrument, _trimBuffer, ask, bid);

// OnFlattenClick (~L764) — BEFORE:
if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || _flattenBuffer == 0)
    _engine.Flatten(_instrument);
else
    _engine.Flatten(_instrument, _flattenBuffer, ask, bid);

// AFTER:
if (ask <= 0 || bid <= 0 || _flattenBuffer == 0)
    _engine.Flatten(_leaderAccount, _instrument);
else
    _engine.Flatten(_leaderAccount, _instrument, _flattenBuffer, ask, bid);

// OnCancel2 (~L912) — BEFORE:
if (_instrument != null) _engine.CancelPendingEntries(_instrument);
// AFTER:
if (_instrument != null) _engine.CancelPendingEntries(_leaderAccount, _instrument);
