B30 DEFECT REGISTER
ID P Root Cause & Fix Status
DW-B30-01 P0 MoveStopToBreakEven cancel+replace partial failure — account left with no stop.
acc.Cancel() succeeds (B29 fix), then acc.CreateOrder() throws → exception caught and swallowed → position unprotected.
Fix: Add retry: up to 3 CreateOrder attempts on failure. On final failure, attempt rollback by re-placing the original stop at order.StopPrice and emit "CRITICAL: PTT-BE rollback: {ex}" StatusUpdate.
Files: CopyEngine.cs:1278-1298. Same fix applies to TightenOneStop:1392-1411. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-02 P1 TightenStop has no leader-direct path — fully blocked without a rule.
TightenStop(Instrument, int) opens with FindRule() → hard return if null. Unlike Trim/Flatten/Cancel/BE (B28 fix), Tighten was never given a leader-direct overload. TightenOneStop is 100% rule-independent internally — it just needs account+order.
Fix (B28 pattern exactly):
1. Add TightenStop(Account leader, Instrument instr, int ticks) overload in CopyEngine.cs.
2. Add private helper TightenOneAccountStops(Account, Instrument, int, double) — extracts the per-account stop-walk from existing TightenStop, adds if (order.Instrument != instrument) continue instrument filter (fixes existing edge-case bug).
3. Update OnTightenStop in TradeCopierPanel.cs to call _engine.TightenStop(_leaderAccount, _instrument, ticks).
Files: CopyEngine.cs:1333+; TradeCopierPanel.cs:942-950. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-03 P1 WireLeaderAccount timing miss — _leaderAccount null at button press.
WireLeaderAccount runs at panel inject time. If the ChartTrader account combo has no SelectedItem yet (user hasn't touched the combo, NT8 not fully initialized), the text-fallback lookup also fails → _leaderAccount stays null. Buttons silently no-op.
Additionally, the SelectionChanged lambda captures panel and is never unsubscribed in Detach() — memory leak on chart close.
Fix:
1. Add TryResolveLeaderAccount() private method in TradeCopierPanel — walks ChartTrader visual tree at call time to find current account combo selection (same logic as WireLeaderAccount).
2. All button handlers: var acc = _leaderAccount ?? TryResolveLeaderAccount(); if (acc == null) return;
3. Make FindAccountComboBox and FindVisualChildByIndex in TradeCopierAddOn.cs internal static (from private) so TradeCopierPanel can call them.
4. Store SelectionChanged handler delegate in panel field; unsubscribe in Detach().
Files: TradeCopierPanel.cs:381-402, 734, 760, 790, 909, 942; TradeCopierAddOn.cs:492, 512, 444-468. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-04 P1 TightenStop MarketData null-dereference crash on untracked instruments.
instrument.MarketData.Bid.Price at CopyEngine.cs:1348 throws NullReferenceException if MarketData or Bid is null (instrument not subscribed to data feed).
Fix: var bidPrice = instrument.MarketData?.Bid?.Price ?? 0.0; and same for Ask. Existing bidPrice > 0 && askPrice > 0 fallback logic then handles the zero case correctly.
Files: CopyEngine.cs:1348-1349. Also apply to new TightenOneAccountStops helper. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-05 P1 ArmPendingBe armed when flat — silent no-op, FSM stuck Armed.
ArmPendingBe() does not validate that the leader has an open position. Button changes to "BE Armed" visually, but OnPendingBeAccountUpdate never fires a P&L-threshold crossing → PendingBeFired never fires → FSM never transitions to Connected → stops never move. User is confused.
Fix: In ArmPendingBe(), check FindPosition(masterAcc, instr) at arm time. If flat, emit StatusUpdate("PTT-BE: no open position") and return without arming. Also: emit StatusUpdate on ArmPendingBe() masterAcc == null path (currently silent).
Files: CopyEngine.cs:1420-1431. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-06 P1 acc.Orders concurrent iteration — not snapshotted before loop.
CancelOneAccount, MoveStopToBreakEven, and TightenStop iterate acc.Orders directly. NT8's OnOrderUpdate fires on a background thread and can modify the collection mid-loop → iterator corruption, missed cancels, or double-processes in high-activity scenarios.
Fix: Snapshot before each loop: var orders = acc.Orders.ToList(); then iterate orders. Apply to CopyEngine.cs:1050, 1255, 1356.
Files: CopyEngine.cs:1050, 1255, 1356. 		CLOSED
DW-B30-07 P2 Rename "Apply Rule" → "Add Followers" + status text clarification.
Current label implies the button is a required prerequisite. It is not — Trim/Flatten/Cancel/BE work without it. The button only registers follower accounts for multi-account fan-out.
Fix:
1. TradeCopierPanel.cs:483 — Content = "Apply Rule" → Content = "Add Followers".
2. TradeCopierPanel.cs:504 — status text initial value: "No instrument" → "Open chart — Trim/Flatten/Cancel/BE ready".
3. TradeCopierPanel.cs:378 — SetInstrument status: "Ready: " + instrument.FullName → "Ready: " + instrument.FullName + " — select followers to copy".
4. TradeCopierPanel.cs:920 — collapse header: "▼ PTT" → "▼ Position Tools".

B30 ARCHITECTURE DECISIONS — LOCKED (Director: go)
  • All 5 action buttons work with zero setup. No rule, no followers, no Apply Rule / Add Followers click required. The ChartTrader account selector alone is sufficient.
  • Follower fan-out is additive and optional. If a rule exists, buttons fan out to followers after operating on the leader. If no rule, leader-only mode silently succeeds — no error, no warning.
  • Leader resolution order (button press time): (1) _leaderAccount if already wired; (2) TryResolveLeaderAccount() visual-tree fallback; (3) if still null, emit StatusUpdate "no account selected" and return.
  • B28 pattern extended to Tighten: TightenStop(Account, Instrument, int) overload follows identical Flatten/Trim/Cancel leader-direct + AllAccounts fan-out structure. CYC=5. Instrument-filter bug fixed in helper.
  • cancel+replace robustness: Both MoveStopToBreakEven and TightenOneStop gain a 3-retry CreateOrder loop with rollback-to-original on final failure.
  • JS-021: No lock(). All new engine methods UI-thread initiated. Orders snapshots via .ToList() before loop — safe, no lock required.
  • CYC impact: All new/modified methods ≤8. New overloads CYC=4-5. Helper CYC=5. Retry loops: factored into callers (each branch counted).
  • [Fact] target: 137 → 142. T_B30_01: TightenStop(Account,Instrument,int) overload exists. T_B30_02: TightenStop(Account,null,5) no-throw. T_B30_03: TrimLimit_Long_PlacesBelowBid (already shipped). T_B30_04: ArmPendingBe null-position no-arm guard. T_B30_05: TryResolveLeaderAccount method exists (reflection).
